	// Google Analytics Event Tracking
	$(document).ready(function(){
		var gaLabel = '';
		$('#advanced-search').click(function(){
			gaLabel = $('#adv_words').val();
			$(this).attr('data-ga-label', gaLabel);
		});
		$('#basic-search').click(function(){
			gaLabel = $('#words').val();
			$(this).attr('data-ga-label', gaLabel);
		});
		$('#contact-button').click(function(){
			gaLabel = $('.fieldContainers select[name="destination_index"] option:selected').html();
			$(this).attr('data-ga-label', gaLabel);
		});
		$('#register').click(function(){
			var gaLabelRaw = $('input[name="sysUseCode"]:checked').val();
			if (gaLabelRaw == 'BU') {
				gaLabel = 'Buyer';
			} else if (gaLabelRaw == 'SE') {
				gaLabel = 'Seller';
			} else {
				gaLabel = 'Unknown';
			}
			$(this).attr('data-ga-label', gaLabel);
		});
		$('.track-ga').gaTrackEventUnobtrusive({
			useEvent: true,
			useLabel: true,
			event: 'click'
		});
		$('.track-ga-load').gaTrackEventUnobtrusive({
			useEvent: false,
			useLabel: true
		});
	});

<script>
/**********************************************/
/* LCOM New Visitor/Logged Banner Display */
/**********************************************/
$(function() {
    if ( $('.sign-Details:contains("Welcome")').length > 0 ) {
        $('.new-visitor-homepage').css('display','block');
        $('.registered-visitor-homepage').css('display','none');
    } else {
        $('.new-visitor-homepage').css('display','none');
        $('.registered-visitor-homepage').css('display','block');
    }
});
</script>
